	{
		IConnectionPoint ppCP = null;
		Guid riid = new Guid(new byte[16]
		{
			50, 214, 63, 80, 62, 246, 209, 17, 141, 62,

		m_ConnectionPoint = ppCP;
		m_aEventSinkHelpers = new ArrayList();
	}

	{
		bool lockTaken = default(bool);

			int pdwCookie = 0;

			iAPHappEvents_SinkHelper.m_dwCookie = pdwCookie;
			iAPHappEvents_SinkHelper.m_OnDialogSuppressedDelegate = P_0;

		finally

				Monitor.Exit(this);
